# Licenze delle dipendenze

File generato da `cargo about generate -c about.toml about.hbs -o THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md`
(configurazione in `about.toml`): non modificarlo a mano.

Questo report raccoglie le licenze dei crate Rust selezionati da cargo-about
secondo `Cargo.lock` e `about.toml`. Non è un inventario dei soli componenti
compilati in un particolare eseguibile: target e feature possono differire.
Icone, font e componenti modificati sono descritti in `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md`.
Quando un pacchetto include i language server, contiene anche i relativi
avvisi; consultare README e metadati del pacchetto per il contenuto effettivo.

## Riepilogo

- MIT License (540 crate)
- zlib License (24 crate)
- Apache License 2.0 (21 crate)
- Unicode License v3 (19 crate)
- ISC License (7 crate)
- BSD 3-Clause "New" or "Revised" License (6 crate)
- Boost Software License 1.0 (2 crate)
- Creative Commons Zero v1.0 Universal (2 crate)
- BSD 2-Clause "Simplified" License (1 crate)
- Community Data License Agreement Permissive 2.0 (1 crate)
- MIT No Attribution (1 crate)
- Mozilla Public License 2.0 (1 crate)

/// The PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_PUA: [u16; 18] = [
    0xE78D, 0xE78E, 0xE78F, 0xE790, 0xE791, 0xE792, 0xE793, 0xE794, 0xE795, 0xE796, 0xE81E, 0xE826,
    0xE82B, 0xE82C, 0xE832, 0xE843, 0xE854, 0xE864,
];

/// The bytes corresponding to the PUA code points special-cased in the GB18030 encoder.
pub(crate) static GB18030_2022_OVERRIDE_BYTES: [[u8; 2]; 18] = [
    [0xA6, 0xD9],
    [0xA6, 0xDA],
    [0xA6, 0xDB],
    [0xA6, 0xDC],
    [0xA6, 0xDD],
    [0xA6, 0xDE],
    [0xA6, 0xDF],
    [0xA6, 0xEC],
    [0xA6, 0xED],
    [0xA6, 0xF3],
    [0xFE, 0x59],
    [0xFE, 0x61],
    [0xFE, 0x66],
    [0xFE, 0x67],
    [0xFE, 0x6D],
    [0xFE, 0x7E],
    [0xFE, 0x90],
    [0xFE, 0xA0],
];

## Licenses that cargo-about cannot resolve automatically

The crates below declare MIT in their manifest but ship no license file in the
published package, or declare a repository URL that cargo-about 0.9.2 cannot
turn into a raw file URL. cargo-about therefore keeps them in the generic MIT
section above, whose copyright line is a placeholder: that section names the
crate but attributes nothing. The notices below are their attribution. Each
quotes the upstream source at the commit the published package was built from
and gives its SHA-256.

### tree-sitter-sequel 0.3.11 (MIT)

Source: https://github.com/derekstride/tree-sitter-sql, commit
7b51ecda191d36b92f5a90a8d1bc3faef1c7b8b8, file `LICENSE`, SHA-256
`3b3e4e4252d5d7d1c18e1257005f23242bf0580ad619204fd093c99b8c56748d`. The
manifest declares a repository URL ending in `.git`, which cargo-about cannot
fetch, and the package ships no license file.

```text

Copyright (c) 2021 Derek Stride

Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy
of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal
in the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights
to use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell
copies of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is
furnished to do so, subject to the following conditions:

The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all
copies or substantial portions of the Software.

THE SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED "AS IS", WITHOUT WARRANTY OF ANY KIND, EXPRESS OR
IMPLIED, INCLUDING BUT NOT LIMITED TO THE WARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY,

AUTHORS OR COPYRIGHT HOLDERS BE LIABLE FOR ANY CLAIM, DAMAGES OR OTHER
LIABILITY, WHETHER IN AN ACTION OF CONTRACT, TORT OR OTHERWISE, ARISING FROM,

SOFTWARE.
```

### block 0.1.6, dispatch 0.2.0 and malloc_buf 0.0.6 (MIT)

None of the three published packages ships a license file, and none of the
tagged repositories has one either: block tag `0.1.6` (commit
`47178790cfc9d4a8b092051d8b413b78bd31254a`), dispatch tag `0.2.0` (commit
`82d6c7a5b75dc0c71c3f46f87bb6c16a476f7748`), malloc_buf tag `0.0.6` (commit
`a7811e5f4c6f9685dd57ad9bfe34e9bf6b0ba4f9`). Their manifests declare
`license = "MIT"` and name Steven Sheldon as author. malloc_buf's repository
later added a `LICENSE` (commit `d9a3e539642bd90e07df458d226b19cdfa606863`,
2020-06-29, SHA-256
`aced7b57e474a63edb5a724954222bf7659c01ceef0e648a03af799582991db7`) with the
notice below. It is the only license text any of the three repositories has
published, and its holder is the author named in all three manifests.
